### [1] [Scaling Inference Prefill with High-Radix Photonic Interconnects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01821)
*Arulselvan Madhavan,Peter Carson,Taylor Groves,Thomas Graham*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文量化了3D集成光子互连在大语言模型推理prefill阶段（尤其是Mixture-of-Experts模型）中的优势，通过模拟三种不同上下文长度的MoE模型，对比铜基GPU系统与光子集成系统，结果显示在压力下延迟改善2.1-3.2倍，通信受限配置下提升2.8-5.8倍，可支持1152-GPU规模并带来2.2-4.5倍速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着推理成为当今AI的主导工作负载，行业正在向高带宽光子互连过渡以满足日益复杂的MoE模型的大规模扩展需求。然而，对于高并发的LLM聊天场景与大型上下文窗口（推理和智能体AI典型需求）之间的权衡尚缺乏定量分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过模拟三种MoE模型（短上下文1K-8K token、中等上下文128K token、长上下文1M token），对比现有基于铜互连的GPU系统与集成高带宽光子互连的系统，分析推理prefill阶段高并发吞吐与大上下文窗口之间的权衡。

Result: 在高批次压力场景下实现2.1-3.2倍延迟改善；在通信受限配置下相对基线提升2.8-5.8倍；3D光子技术使1152-GPU规模成为可能，在电气系统突破其固有scale-up-pod限制时，跨生产级平台实现2.2-4.5倍加速。

Conclusion: 3D集成光子互连在MoE推理prefill阶段具有显著优势，能有效突破电气互连的扩展限制，尤其在高并发和长上下文场景下大幅缩短首token时间（TTFT），是下一代AI推理基础设施的关键技术方向。

Abstract: With the rise of inference as today's dominant AI workload, the industry is transitioning to high-bandwidth photonic interconnects to meet the large scale-up requirements of increasingly complex Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. This paper quantifies the benefits of 3D-integrated photonic interconnects for inference prefill by analyzing tradeoffs between high-concurrency throughput for Large Language Model (LLM) chat and the large context windows typically required for reasoning and agentic AI. We simulate three MoE models: short context (1K--8K tokens), medium context (128K tokens), and long context (1M tokens). We evaluate this workload across existing copper-based GPU systems and one with high bandwidth integrated photonics. We show 2.1--3.2x latency improvements in the stressed high-batch regimes and 2.8--5.8x improvements over baselines in communication-limited configurations. 3D photonics enable the 1152-GPU footprint required to lower time-to-first-token, yielding 2.2--4.5x speedups across production-grade platforms when electrical systems cross their inherent scale-up-pod limits.

</details>


### [2] [CREDIT: Cost-guided Reduction-reuse with Efficient DSMEM Inter-CTA Tiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01864)
*Zhengxiong Li,Tsung-Wei Huang,Umit Ogras*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CREDIT框架，用于判断GPU线程块簇中NVIDIA分布式共享内存(DSMEM)何时能提升性能。CREDIT通过剖析驱动的特征识别、DSMEM变换和成本模型三个创新，在多样工作负载上达到91.7%的盈利预测准确率，并在RTX 5090和H100上分别实现1.466x和1.318x的几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVIDIA分布式共享内存(DSMEM)允许线程块簇内直接共享内存访问，但簇同步、远程访问和资源开销使其性能收益难以预判，缺乏系统化的判断方法。

Method: CREDIT结合三种创新：(1)基于剖析驱动的特征表征，识别可能从DSMEM中获益的工作负载模式；(2)针对reduction-reuse工作负载应用DSMEM的变换方法；(3)基于剖析数据的成本模型，用于确定DSMEM的盈利范围。

Result: 在多样工作负载上，CREDIT达到91.7%的盈利预测准确率；在全部六个工作负载上超越torch.compile、Triton和优化的非DSMEM CUDA基线，在RTX 5090上实现1.466x几何平均加速，在H100上实现1.318x几何平均加速。

Conclusion: CREDIT作为首个成本引导的DSMEM盈利性判别框架，有效填补了DSMEM性能收益难以判断的空白，为GPU编程提供了实用的自动优化指导，且源码已开源。

Abstract: NVIDIA distributed shared memory (DSMEM) enables direct shared-memory access within a thread block cluster. However, cluster synchronization, remote access, and resource costs make it difficult to determine when DSMEM improves performance. To fill this gap, we propose CREDIT, a cost-guided framework that identifies DSMEM-profitable workload patterns, predicts their profitability range, and delivers consistent speedups across diverse workloads. CREDIT combines three innovations: (1) a profiling-driven characterization that identifies workload patterns likely to benefit from DSMEM; (2) a transformation that applies DSMEM to reduction-reuse workloads; (3) a cost model based on profiling data, to determine its profitability range. Evaluations on diverse workloads show CREDIT achieves 91.7% prediction accuracy on profitability. CREDIT beats torch.compile, Triton, and optimized non-DSMEM CUDA baselines on all six workloads, with geometric-mean speedups of 1.466x on RTX 5090 and 1.318x on H100. CREDIT's source code is publicly available at https://github.com/zhengxiongli08/CREDIT.

</details>


### [3] [RT-HiSS: Ray Tracing Accelerated High Dimensional Vector Similarity Searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01975)
*Revanth Reddy Munugala,Michael Gowanlock*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RT-HiSS是首个基于GPU RT核心的高维数据集精确相似度搜索算法，利用RT核心进行快速索引构建和搜索，并在CUDA核心上进行候选精炼，在六个真实数据集上相比最先进的GPU算法最高加速8.37倍，相比暴力搜索最高加速2368倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GPU RT核心的相似度搜索方法仅支持三维以内的数据集，无法应对高维数据的相似度搜索需求。

Method: RT-HiSS提出RT核心与CUDA核心协同的精确相似度搜索框架：使用RT核心进行快速索引构建与搜索，结合两遍扫描方法估计结果大小的上界，实现GPU内存约束下的高效批处理与接近完美的负载均衡；同时采用共享内存平铺和压缩结果掩码优化GPU资源利用率。

Result: 在六个真实数据集上，RT-HiSS相比最先进的GPU算法最高获得8.37倍加速，相对暴力搜索算法最高获得2368.26倍加速。

Conclusion: RT-HiSS证明了GPU RT核心可有效拓展至高维相似度搜索场景，通过RT核心加速搜索与CUDA核心精炼候选的混合架构，显著提升了大规模高维数据集的相似度搜索性能。

Abstract: Recent GPU generations include special-purpose ray tracing (RT) cores for graphics applications. While RT cores are primarily used for rendering, recent works show they can be leveraged for general-purpose tasks, including similarity searches. However, existing approaches do not support datasets exceeding three dimensions. In this work, we propose RT-HiSS, the first exact GPU RT-core-based similarity search algorithm for high-dimensional datasets. GPU similarity search often scales poorly for large datasets with substantial search distances. To address this, RT-HiSS uses RT cores for fast index construction and searches, followed by candidate refinement on CUDA cores. We introduce a two-pass approach to estimate an upper bound on result size, enabling efficient batching under GPU memory constraints with near-perfect load balancing. Additionally, we examine shared memory tiling and compressed result masks to improve GPU resource utilization. RT-HiSS yields speedups up to 8.37$\times$ over competitive state-of-the-art GPU algorithms and up to 2,368.26$\times$ relative to the brute-force algorithm across six real-world datasets.

</details>


### [4] [MeanField Surrogate Modeling for Scalable Runtime Scheduling of Concurrent Heterogeneous AI Inference on Shared GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02109)
*Youssef Ennouri,Soonhoi Ha*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种MeanField代理模型，通过局部配置和聚合GPU状态来预测异构AI模型并发运行时的性能，避免了对所有联合交互的显式建模，使采样预算从组合级增长降为近似线性增长，并集成到遗传算法调度器中实现高效且无SLA违规的调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享GPU上并发部署异构AI模型会产生资源争用，导致运行时调度复杂化。虽然代理模型可以避免昂贵的在线基准测试，但其配置需求通常随共运行模型数量呈组合级增长，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 提出MeanField代理模型，仅从局部配置和聚合GPU状态预测每个模型的性能，而不是显式建模所有联合交互关系。将该模型集成到遗传算法调度器中，用于搜索最优的联合配置方案。

Result: 在N∈{2,3,4,5,6}的并发LLM和视觉负载实验中，预测精度R²≈0.96，采样预算近似线性增长。在N=5问题（78,732种可行联合配置）中，调度结果与穷举搜索差距在0.10%以内，零SLA违规，完整在线GA决策中位耗时26ms，比穷举代理搜索快约5倍。

Conclusion: MeanField代理模型有效解决了异构AI并发调度中联合建模的组合爆炸问题，在保持高预测精度的同时将采样成本降至线性水平，与遗传算法结合后能够在复杂配置空间中快速找到接近最优的调度方案，且满足SLA要求。

Abstract: Deploying heterogeneous AI models concurrently on a shared GPU introduces resource contention that complicates runtime scheduling. While surrogate models avoid costly online benchmarking, their profiling requirements typically grow combinatorially with the number of co-running models, limiting scalability. We propose a MeanField surrogate that predicts per-model performance from local configuration and aggregate GPU state rather than explicitly modeling all joint interactions. Experiments on concurrent LLM and vision workloads across $N \in \{2,3,4,5,6\}$ show high predictive accuracy ($R^2 \approx 0.96$) with an empirical sample budget that grows approximately linearly in $N$, in contrast to the combinatorial cost of fully joint profiling. Integrated into a genetic algorithm scheduler, the surrogate scales to an $N=5$ problem with 78,732 feasible joint configurations, remaining within 0.10% of the exhaustive search with zero SLA violations across eight dynamic workload scenarios, while complete online GA decisions take 26 ms median, about $5\times$ faster than exhaustive surrogate search.

</details>


### [5] [Towards Global Federated Genome-Wide Association Meta-Analysis Using GA4GH TES](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02227)
*Abhijit Chunduru,Matthew Joel,Zilinghan Li,Ravi Madduri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于APPFL框架的隐私保护联邦GWAS荟萃分析流水线，各站点仅共享汇总统计量而非个体基因型数据，通过GA4GH标准化的TES端点实现跨机构/跨境分布式计算，并在十万合成个体的模拟中验证了其与集中式分析等效的关联信号复现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GWAS需要大规模、多祖先人群样本以提高统计功效，但隐私法规和数据驻留限制阻碍了基因组数据的跨机构或跨国集中汇聚，亟需在不共享个体级数据的前提下实现大规模联合分析的基础设施。

Method: 基于APPFL框架构建隐私保护联邦GWAS流程：各站点计算本地GWAS汇总统计量（仅传输聚合结果）；通过GA4GH TES全球端点网络执行分布式分析（计算移至数据端）；服务器采用逆方差加权固定效应荟萃分析并返回聚合结果；另开发HiveWatch地理可观测性工具包实现分布式任务执行的实时监控。

Result: 在5站点、10万合成个体、约24万个变体的模拟中（针对2型糖尿病和BMI性状），联邦荟萃分析成功复现了集中式合并分析预期的关联信号，且未集中任何基因型数据。

Conclusion: 基于标准的任务执行（TES）与联邦学习相结合，能够构建实用且隐私保护的国际化GWAS荟萃分析基础设施，为跨机构、跨国的基因组研究提供了可行的去中心化方案。

Abstract: Genome-wide association studies (GWAS) gain statistical power from large, ancestrally diverse cohorts, but privacy regulations and data-residency constraints often prevent genomic data from being centrally pooled across institutional or national borders. We present a privacy-preserving federated GWAS meta-analysis pipeline built on the APPFL framework, in which each site computes local GWAS summary statistics and transmits only aggregate results, never individual-level genotypes. Analysis is executed through a global network of Global Alliance for Genomics and Health (GA4GH) Task Execution Service (TES) endpoints, which allows computation to move to the data rather than the reverse. The server performs inverse-variance-weighted fixed-effect meta-analysis and returns aggregated results to all sites, while HiveWatch, our developed geographic observability toolkit, provides real-time monitoring of distributed task execution. In a five-site simulation over 100,000 synthetic individuals and roughly 240,000 variants for Type 2 Diabetes and Body Mass Index, the federated meta-analysis reproduces the association signal expected from a pooled analysis without centralizing any genotype data, showing that standards-based task execution and federated learning enables a practical privacy-preserving infrastructure for international GWAS meta-analysis.

</details>


### [6] [Federated Learning on the American Science Cloud using APPFL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02238)
*Zilinghan Li,Abhijit Chunduru,Harinarayan Krishnan,Eric Chagnon,Peter Nugent,Kibaek Kim,Ravi Madduri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出将APPFL联邦学习框架的编排逻辑部署为美国科学云（AmSC）上的可扩展云服务，利用AmSC已有的原语（认证、函数执行、实验跟踪、模型托管）来实现跨组织边界的隐私保护模型训练，从而解锁受隐私约束的科学协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AmSC的早期服务缺乏跨组织边界的模型训练能力，而由于政策、隐私或规模限制，数据无法集中化处理。这构成了联邦学习（FL）的典型应用场景，是科学AI中日益增长的一类需求。

Method: 将高级隐私保护联邦学习（APPFL）框架的编排逻辑部署为AmSC上的可扩展云服务，利用AmSC已提供的原语：项目级认证支持联邦成员关系、函数执行驱动各站点的分布式训练、实验跟踪记录每轮性能、模型托管和推理基础设施用于向授权参与者分发联邦训练模型。

Result: 证明了通过部署APPFL云服务可以弥合AmSC在跨组织联邦训练能力上的空白，将联邦计算作为AmSC的重要服务能够解锁隐私受限的科学协作场景，并支持公私合作伙伴关系中的模型共建。

Conclusion: 将联邦计算作为AmSC的一项核心服务，既能为隐私约束下的科学协作提供关键能力，又能锻炼和增强平台自身的联邦基础设施。

Abstract: The American Science Cloud (AmSC), established under the Genesis Mission of the U.S. Department of Energy (DOE), aims to integrate DOE high-performance computing systems, experimental facilities, and data resources into a single, coordinated, AI-driven discovery platform. AmSC's early services focus on curated artifacts, such as gated inference access to hosted models, experiment tracking, and function execution across computing facilities. However, what these services lack is a means to train a model across organizational boundaries where data cannot be centralized due to policy, privacy, or scale. This is, by definition, a use case for federated learning (FL) and a growing class of scientific AI. In this paper, we show that this gap can be bridged by deploying the orchestration logic of the Advanced Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning (APPFL) framework as a scalable cloud service on top of the primitives AmSC already provides: project-scoped authentication that supports secure and reliable federation membership, function execution that drives distributed training at each site, experiment tracking that records round-level performance, and finally, the model-hosting and inference infrastructure that can be leveraged to distribute the federated trained models to authorized participants. We argue that offering federated computing as an important AmSC service would unlock privacy-constrained scientific collaborations, enabling public-private partnerships in model building while exercising and enhancing the platform's own federated infrastructure.

</details>


### [7] [AceSpec: An Asymmetric Edge-Cloud Collaborative Framework for Communication-Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02514)
*Yida Zhang,Zhiyong Gao,Shuaibing Yue,Jie Li,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AceSpec是一个非对称端云协同推理框架，通过在边缘设备上构建概率状态缓存来消除推测解码中因网络波动导致的管道停滞，将网络级回滚转化为O(1)的本地内存查找，并采用非对称通信协议和Lagrangian优化的资源分配策略，实现了最高3.52倍吞吐加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘部署LLM的现有方案存在根本性缺陷：模型压缩会损害推理能力，而拆分推理受限于广域网通信瓶颈。端云协同推测解码虽有望解决此问题，但在波动的WAN环境下，预测被拒绝会引发严重的管道停滞和网络级回滚，抵消了协作带来的收益。

Method: AceSpec框架包含三个核心组件：1) 利用边缘侧未饱和计算能力主动构建概率状态缓存，将网络级管道flush转化为O(1)本地内存查找；2) 非对称通信协议——上行仅传输最小主链索引，下行传输紧凑稀疏分布，以节省带宽；3) 网络感知的Lagrangian优化资源分配策略，动态最大化本地缓存命中率。

Result: 实验评估显示AceSpec实现了最高3.52倍的吞吐加速，并展现出卓越的带宽鲁棒性，即使在严重受限的50 Kbps WAN条件下仍能维持接近峰值的推理性能。

Conclusion: AceSpec通过将网络不确定性转化为本地缓存优势，有效解决了端云协同推测解码在易变网络下的关键瓶颈，为边缘LLM推理提供了高效且带宽鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) on edge devices typically relies on model compression or split inference. However, compression degrades reasoning capabilities, while split inference suffers from severe Wide Area Network (WAN) communication bottlenecks. Edge-cloud speculative decoding emerges as a promising alternative, leveraging an edge small model to draft tokens for cloud verification. Yet, over volatile WANs, inevitable prediction rejections trigger catastrophic pipeline stalls and network-wide rollbacks, neutralizing collaborative gains. To overcome this, we propose AceSpec, an asymmetric edge-cloud collaborative framework. AceSpec utilizes un-saturated edge compute to proactively construct a probabilistic state cache, effectively transforming network-wide pipeline flushes into $\mathcal{O}(1)$ local memory lookups. To preserve bandwidth, it employs an asymmetric communication protocol that transmits minimal main-chain indices uplink and compact sparse distributions downlink. Furthermore, we introduce a network-aware, Lagrangian-optimized resource allocation strategy that dynamically maximizes the local cache hit rate. Evaluations demonstrate that AceSpec achieves up to a 3.52$\times$ throughput speedup and exhibits exceptional bandwidth immunity, sustaining near-peak inference performance even under severely constrained 50 Kbps WAN conditions.

</details>

### [8] [WMLLM: Self-Evolving Optimization Agents via Predict-Then-Act World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01608)
*Zhongzheng Li,Qingsong Ran,Shikun Feng,Nian Ran,Wenhao Li,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yue Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WMLLM框架，一种基于"先预测后行动"世界建模的自进化优化代理框架，通过大语言模型预测有前景的优化方向后再生成候选解，结合多轮细化、种群搜索和强化学习，在尤其是多目标分子优化等黑盒优化任务上显著提升样本效率并达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒优化问题因搜索空间大、结构弱和高维而具有挑战性，现有方法依赖直接候选生成或试错细化导致样本效率低下。大语言模型能凭借隐式知识以非平凡准确度预测候选结果，因此利用世界建模来提升搜索效率是一个自然且有前景的方向。

Method: WMLLM采用"预测后行动"（predict-then-act）的世界建模策略：代理首先预测有前景的优化方向，然后据此生成候选解。框架整合了代理式多轮细化、基于种群的搜索和强化学习，在搜索过程中同时优化隐式世界模型和优化策略，实现自进化。

Result: 在黑盒优化任务（尤其是多目标分子优化基准）上，WMLLM在有限评估预算下提升了样本效率和最终优化性能，并取得了最先进（state-of-the-art）的结果。

Conclusion: 实验验证了基于大语言模型世界建模的"预测后行动"搜索范式能有效提升黑盒优化的样本效率，为利用LLM隐式知识指导优化搜索提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Black-box optimization problems remain challenging because of large, weakly structured, and high-dimensional search spaces. Existing methods often suffer from poor sample efficiency because they rely on direct candidate generation or trial-and-error refinement. A natural way to improve search efficiency is to use world modeling, which can help identify promising optimization directions before costly evaluation. Large language models can predict the outcomes of these candidates with nontrivial accuracy because of their implicit knowledge. Motivated by this observation, we propose WMLLM, a self-evolving optimization-agent framework based on predict-then-act world modeling. The agent first predicts promising directions and then acts to generate candidates. Combined with agentic multi-turn refinement, population-based search, and reinforcement learning, WMLLM refines both its implicit world model and its optimization strategy during search. Experiments on black-box optimization tasks, especially multi-objective molecular optimization, show that WMLLM improves sample efficiency and final optimization performance. On the multi-objective molecular optimization benchmark, WMLLM achieves state-of-the-art results under a limited evaluation budget.

</details>


### [9] [DiDrive: A Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion Framework for Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01609)
*Qisong Guo,Jingtang Chen,Zhilin Chen,Pei Xu,Mingjian Fu,Wenxi Liu,Yuanlong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DiDrive框架，一个分布引导的离线扩散框架，包含风险感知分层扩散（RHDif）架构和3DICE策略优化范式，用于解决自动驾驶中离线RL面临的分布偏移、重尾风险信号、OOD动作生成和高维状态冗余问题。在CARLA基准上，特别是在60辆高密度交通场景中取得85%成功率和4295.68平均奖励。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能有效捕获自动驾驶的多模态行为先验，但离线RL策略仍面临分布偏移、重尾风险信号、OOD动作生成和高维状态冗余等多重挑战，需要更鲁棒的决策框架。

Method: 提出双组件协同的DiDrive框架：1）RHDif（风险感知分层扩散）在状态空间利用低层风险门控编码器和高层上下文调制器，过滤环境冗余并聚焦安全关键威胁；2）3DICE在动作空间通过样本内校准引导、时空优化和集成候选排序来缓解OOD过估计和梯度振荡。

Result: 在CARLA基准上的评估显示，DiDrive优于IQL、CQL和Diffusion-QL等基线方法，尤其在60辆车的高密度交通场景中表现突出，实现85%的成功率和4295.68的平均奖励。

Conclusion: DiDrive为安全自动驾驶决策提供了一条鲁棒路径，证明了分布引导的离线扩散框架在复杂高密度交通场景下的有效性。

Abstract: While diffusion models effectively capture multimodal behavioral priors for autonomous driving, offline reinforcement learning (RL) policies remain susceptible to distribution shift, heavy-tailed risk signals, out-of-distribution (OOD) action generation, and high-dimensional state redundancy. To address these challenges, we propose DiDrive, a distribution-guided offline diffusion framework featuring two synergistic components: the Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion (RHDif) architecture and the 3DICE policy optimization paradigm. In the state space, RHDif utilizes a low-level risk-gated encoder and a high-level contextual modulator to filter environmental redundancy and focus on safety-critical threats. In the action space, 3DICE mitigates OOD overestimation and gradient oscillation through in-sample calibrated guidance, spatiotemporal optimization, and ensemble-based candidate ranking. Evaluations on the CARLA benchmark demonstrate DiDrive's superiority over baselines like IQL, CQL, and Diffusion-QL, particularly in complex, high-density traffic scenarios with 60 vehicles, where it achieves an 85% success rate and a 4295.68 average reward, providing a robust pathway for safe autonomous driving decision-making.

</details>


### [10] [Prompt-Space Meta-Learning Does Not Transfer Across Users: A Frozen-LLM Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01615)
*Liam Byrne,David Dylan,Orla Fitzgerald,Eoin Doyle,Ciara Nolan,Padraig Lynch,Sinead Gallagher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Muse框架质疑"元学习个性化冻结大语言模型"这一普遍假设，发现共享提示的元优化目标实际无法编码跨用户可迁移的适应能力，而是发生"元目标坍缩"——退化为指令润色和验证集过拟合，在LaMP-2和LaMP-3基准上均未优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化冻结大语言模型的元学习框架被广泛采用，但鲜有研究检验元优化目标是否真正编码了可迁移的跨用户适应能力，而非仅仅反映通用指令质量。作者希望通过严格对照实验来检验这一核心假设。

Method: 提出Muse框架，通过反思式提示进化在元训练用户群体上演化单一共享适应提示，冻结后零样本应用于未见用户。设置匹配对照组：未进化的种子提示、结构破坏对照（在不匹配的用户-支持对上元训练）、以及不变性预言机，以分离学习效应与措辞/选择混淆因素。

Result: 在LaMP-2（200个未见用户）和LaMP-3（200个未见用户）基准上，Muse未显著优于其未进化的种子提示或结构破坏对照组；在评分任务上被简单少样本检索显著超越（Delta MAE +0.175, p < 0.001）。元验证目标在用户-支持对应关系是否真实方面统计不变（LaMP-2 p=0.555, LaMP-3 p=0.622）。

Conclusion: 作者提出"元目标坍缩"机制解释实验结果：元验证目标对用户-支持对应关系是否真实不敏感，因此无法被优化为可迁移的适应策略，只会奖励指令润色和验证过拟合。提出的对照协议（种子提示、错误支持、不变性预言机）可复用，用于分离真实学习到的适应与混淆因素。

Abstract: Personalizing a frozen large language model (LLM) to individual users is often framed as a meta-learning problem in prompt space: each user is a task, and one seeks a shared natural-language adaptation policy that, given a handful of the user's labeled interactions, configures the frozen model for that user. The framing is attractive because it is backbone-agnostic and reuses the machinery of prompt optimization, yet the field rarely tests whether the optimized meta-objective encodes transferable cross-user adaptation rather than generic instruction quality. We study this question with Muse (Meta-learned User-adaptation via Shared Evolution), which evolves a single shared adaptation prompt over a meta-train user population by reflective prompt evolution, freezes it, and applies it zero-shot to held-out users; matched controls isolate learning from confounds of phrasing and selection. On two standard personalization benchmarks (LaMP-2 categorization and LaMP-3 rating) over 200 held-out users each, Muse does not significantly improve on its own un-evolved seed prompt or on a structure-broken control that meta-trains on mismatched user-support pairs, and is dominated by plain few-shot retrieval on the rating task (Delta MAE +0.175, p < 0.001). We attribute these outcomes to a single mechanism, meta-objective collapse: the meta-validation objective is statistically invariant to whether the user-support correspondence is genuine (p=0.555 on LaMP-2, p=0.622 on LaMP-3), so it cannot be optimized into transferable adaptation and instead rewards instruction polish and validation overfitting. The seed-prompt, wrong-support, and invariance-oracle controls form a reusable protocol that separates learned adaptation from these confounds.

</details>


### [11] [Efficient Context-Limited Telescope Bibliography Classification for the WASP-2025 Shared Task Using SciBERT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01647)
*Madhusudhana Naidu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SciBERT的高效自动分类方法，用于将科学论文分类为科学、仪器、提及和非望远镜四类，在WASP-2025排行榜上以0.89的macro F1分数名列第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 望远镜书目编制是评估天文台科学影响和确保天文学可重复性的关键环节，但传统上依赖大量人工且资源消耗大，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 采用SciBERT模型对科学论文进行四分类，在严格的512 token上下文长度限制和有限计算资源条件下，分析截断策略对分类效果的影响，并讨论截断、分块和长上下文模型之间的权衡。

Result: 方法取得了0.89的macro F1分数，在WASP-2025排行榜上排名第一。即使超过一半样本超出token限制，SciBERT的领域对齐能力仍能保证稳健的分类性能。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了科学文本策展中效率前沿的洞见，表明在资源受限条件下，领域预训练的短上下文模型通过合理截断也能达到高效且稳健的分类效果。

Abstract: The creation of telescope bibliographies is a crucial part of assessing the scientific impact of observatories and ensuring reproducibility in astronomy. This task involves identifying, categorizing, and linking scientific publications that reference or use specific telescopes. However, this process remains largely manual and resource intensive. In this work, we present an efficient SciBERT-based approach for automatic classification of scientific papers into four categories - science, instrumentation, mention, and not telescope. Despite strict context-length constraints (maximum 512 tokens) and limited compute resources, our approach achieved a macro F1 score of 0.89, ranking at the top of the WASP-2025 leaderboard. We analyze the effect of truncation and show that even with half the samples exceeding the token limit, SciBERT's domain alignment enables robust classification. We discuss trade-offs between truncation, chunking, and long-context models, providing insights into the efficiency frontier for scientific text curation.

</details>


### [12] [CliffRank: A Dual-Branch Framework for Activity-Cliff Ranking Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01673)
*Kewei Li,Rongying Zhang,Peiyu Yang,Zhongjian Wang,Qiuchen Zhao,Lan Huang,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CliffRank方法，结合绝对活性回归与排序一致性学习（包括PPC损失），用于解决活性悬崖排序难题。在三个抗菌肽数据集和三个小分子数据集上，CliffRank分别达到最高平均Spearman相关系数0.5393和0.6890，验证了该方法的有效性，同时揭示了PPC的实用边界与初始化策略等局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性悬崖（Activity-cliff）排序任务非常困难，因为局部结构微小变化会导致活性大幅差异，而能够解析其内在机制的高质量数据又十分有限。因此需要更有效地利用现有活性标签来提升排序性能。

Method: CliffRank训练两个并行预测器，采用均方误差（MSE）、阈值化listwise损失以及Pairwise Preference Consistency（PPC）损失。PPC在偏好概率空间中对齐相对排序。对于小分子数据集使用PNA模型，并在120个epoch后激活PPC；对于抗菌肽数据集使用ESM2-t12作为编码器，同时探索了非对称初始化和延迟PPC调度策略。

Result: 在三个抗菌肽数据集上，CliffRank+ESM2-t12达到最高平均Spearman相关系数0.5393和平均Recall@50为21.4。在三个小分子数据集上，CliffRank+PNA达到最高平均Spearman相关系数0.6890，平均Recall@50为30.4，与ACANet-PNA持平。不过各数据集上领先方法并不完全一致，且非对称初始化不能全面改进，延迟PPC也没有统一最优调度。

Conclusion: CliffRank通过结合回归与排序一致性学习有效提升了活性悬崖排序性能。未来工作应评估更多靶标与抗菌肽系统，开发自适应的PPC调度策略，并在可用时引入蛋白质或膜背景信息。

Abstract: Activity-cliff ranking remains difficult because local structural changes can cause large activity differences, while high-quality data that resolve the underlying mechanisms remain limited. To use available activity labels more effectively, we combine absolute-activity regression with ranking-consistency learning. CliffRank trains two parallel predictors with mean squared error, a thresholded listwise loss, and Pairwise Preference Consistency (PPC), which aligns relative ordering in the preference-probability space. On three antimicrobial peptide datasets, CliffRank with ESM2-t12 achieved the highest mean Spearman correlation of 0.5393 and mean Recall@50 of 21.4, although the leading method varied across individual datasets. On three small-molecule datasets, CliffRank with PNA, where PPC was activated after 120 epochs, achieved the highest mean Spearman correlation of 0.6890, while its mean Recall@50 of 30.4 matched that of ACANet-PNA. The PPC results also define its practical limits. Asymmetric initialization improved the MolCLR-GIN averages but did not improve every target. For PNA without pretrained weights, delayed PPC improved selected metrics, but no schedule was best for both mean Spearman correlation and mean Recall@50. Future work should evaluate more targets and antimicrobial peptide systems, develop adaptive PPC schedules, and incorporate protein or membrane context when available.

</details>


### [13] [Sim2Signal: Sim-to-Real Benchmarks for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01676)
*Ferdous Al Rafi,Susrik Mukherjee,Latika Liladhar Dekate,Jennifer Yawa Lavoe,Huaiyuan Yao,Shlok Mohanty,Longchao Da,Xuesong Zhou,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sim2Signal基准，系统分解交通信号控制中强化学习的Sim-to-Real差距为观测、动作、转移和奖励四种缺口，在33种设置和10个真实网络下评估了18种缓解方法，发现直接迁移在所有缺口下均性能下降，且缓解方法的有效性高度依赖网络和设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在仿真中表现优异的交通信号控制策略部署到真实世界时常失败（Sim-to-Real gap），但现有研究未充分理解各差距来源的相对影响，也缺少标准基准来系统测量差距和评估缓解方法。

Method: 提出Sim2Signal基准，将Sim-to-Real差距按MDP四要素分解为观测、动作、转移和奖励四类缺口，在共享协议下单独诱发各类缺口；在2个基础控制器、33种缺口设置、10个基于5个真实地点构建的校准网络上评估18种缓解方法。

Result: 直接迁移在所有四类缺口下均导致性能下降；但性能退化严重程度不能预测缓解方法的有效性。缓解效果强烈依赖网络和缺口设置（动作缺口之外，某方法有效可能在另一场景失效）。最有效的方法通常是估计缺口带来的变化，而非通过域随机化或不变表示使策略不敏感。

Conclusion: Sim2Signal揭示了交通信号控制中Sim-to-Real问题的高度情境依赖性，指出针对缺口建模的方法优于域随机化等保守策略，为领域提供了标准化评估基准。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning achieves strong traffic signal control performance in simulation, yet policies trained in simulators often fail once deployed in the real world, a failure known as the Sim-to-Real gap. When RL is applied to traffic signal control, this gap arises from several sources: sensing, action execution, traffic dynamics, and the control objective. Their relative impact and the reliability of existing Sim-to-Real mitigation methods remain insufficiently understood, and the field lacks a standard benchmark for systematically measuring the gap and evaluating mitigation methods. We present Sim2Signal, a benchmark that decomposes the Sim-to-Real gap into observation, action, transition, and reward gaps, corresponding to mismatches in the four components of the underlying MDP, and induces each gap in isolation under a shared protocol. We evaluate 18 mitigation methods on 2 base controllers, across 33 gap settings and 10 calibrated networks built from 5 real-world locations. We find that direct transfer consistently degrades performance across all four gap sources, but the severity of the degradation does not predict the effectiveness of mitigation. Instead, mitigation effectiveness depends strongly on the network and gap setting: outside the action gap, a method that helps in one case may fail in another. The most effective methods generally estimate what the gap changes, rather than make the policy insensitive through domain randomization or invariant representations. Our code is available at https://github.com/Red-Pheonix/Sim2RealTSCBenchMark

</details>


### [14] [A Survey on Self-Improving Test-Time Intelligence: Feedback-Driven Adapting, Learning, and Scaling at Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01679)
*Shuaicheng Niu,Guohao Chen,Yaofo Chen,Zhiquan Wen,Jinwu Hu,Zeshuai Deng,Deyu Chen,Shuhai Zhang,Renjie Chen,Zihao Lian,Shoukai Xu,Gang Dai,Yunbei Zhang,Wei Luo,Yifan Zhang,Mingkui Tan,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"反馈驱动的测试时智能"(TTI)的统一视角，用于理解AI系统在部署/推理阶段通过测试时信息和额外计算进行自我改进的现象，将测试时适应、测试时学习和测试时扩展三大研究方向整合为一个统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着推理超越静态的固定模型执行，AI系统在部署期间提升自身行为的能力日益重要。然而相关研究沿着两条独立方向（基于测试时信号修改模型状态的方法，以及通过额外推理资源改善预测的方法）发展，各自使用不同术语，导致它们之间的联系难以被看清，研究社区之间缺乏统一的交流框架。

Method: 通过综述性方法，提出"反馈驱动的测试时智能"(TTI)作为统一视角，将测试时适应、测试时学习和测试时扩展三者关联起来，梳理其区别与混合系统中的相互重叠，并系统回顾主要方法论范式、代表性应用及开放挑战。

Result: 论文成功构建了一个统一的TTI框架，将之前碎片化的想法联系起来，涵盖视觉、语言、多模态学习、生成模型、机器人及医疗等领域的应用全景，为推理时自我改进研究提供了清晰的概念基础和路线图。

Conclusion: TTI为研究测试时自我改进的AI系统提供了连贯的概念基础和未来的研究路线图，有助于连接不同社区中表面上分离的研究方向，推动该领域的系统性发展。

Abstract: The ability of AI systems to improve their behavior during deployment is becoming increasingly important. As inference moves beyond the static execution of a fixed trained model, a growing body of work studies how models can refine their behavior on the fly by exploiting test-time information and additional computation. These developments have largely evolved along two directions: methods that modify the model's state using test-time signals, and methods that improve predictions through extra inference-time resources such as more sampling and tool use. However, these directions are often studied in separate communities with different terminology, making their connections harder to see. In this survey, we present feedback-driven Test-Time Intelligence (TTI) as a unified perspective for understanding such deployment-time improvement. We use this view to relate test-time adaptation, test-time learning, and test-time scaling, highlighting both their distinctions and their growing overlap in hybrid systems. This unified framework helps connect previously fragmented ideas and provides a clearer conceptual foundation for studying inference-time self-improvement. We review major methodological paradigms, representative applications, and open challenges across vision, language, multimodal learning, generative models, robotics, and healthcare. Our goal is to provide a coherent foundation and research roadmap for the study of self-improving AI systems at test time.

</details>


### [15] [CACTUS: Mask-Guided Semantic Clean-Label Backdoors in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02450)
*Chao Feng,Burkhard Stiller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CACTUS是一种针对去中心化联邦学习(DFL)的语义后门攻击方法，通过将标签一致的语义对转换为目标定向的表示偏移，并利用掩码引导的模态特定算子将触发效应耦合到干净样本嵌入上，在四种模态、九种聚合规则下实现了高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的语义触发比合成补丁更隐蔽，但样本依赖的放置方式可能削弱后门在跨轮聚合中的植入效果；这一问题在去中心化联邦学习中更为严峻，因为拓扑依赖的节点间聚合会反复混合本地模型，使得现有攻击方法效果受限。

Method: CACTUS将标签一致的语义对转化为目标定向的表示偏移；使用掩码引导的模态特定算子来隔离触发效应，并将它们跨样本耦合，然后以反事实方式将这些偏移应用于干净的、非目标的嵌入，之后再执行节点间聚合。

Result: 实验覆盖语音、文本、表格和图像任务，在九种聚合规则下进行测试。在30%恶意节点的情况下，CACTUS在Speech Commands上达到九种规则的平均攻击成功率(ASR)为51.2%，并在四种模态中的三种上取得最高的九规则平均ASR。敏感性分析表明ASR随网络拓扑变化，并随恶意节点比例增加而上升。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明CACTUS能够通过重复的DFL聚合传播后门，验证了其在不同聚合规则和多种数据模态下的有效性。

Abstract: Semantic triggers in federated learning (FL) can be less conspicuous than synthetic patches, but sample-dependent placement may weaken backdoor implantation across aggregation rounds. This challenge is compounded in decentralized FL (DFL), where topology-dependent peer aggregation repeatedly mixes local models. CACTUS converts label-consistent semantic pairs into target-directed representation shifts. Mask-guided, modality-specific operators isolate trigger effects, couple them across samples, and apply the shifts counterfactually to clean non-target embeddings before peer aggregation. Experiments cover speech, text, tabular, and image tasks under nine aggregation rules. With 30\% malicious nodes, CACTUS reaches a nine-rule mean attack success rate (ASR) of 51.2\% on Speech Commands and the highest nine-rule mean ASR among evaluated attacks on three of four modalities. Sensitivity analyses show that ASR varies with network topology and increases with the malicious-node ratio. These results indicate that CACTUS can propagate backdoors through repeated DFL aggregation.

</details>


### [16] [Reinforcement Learning and Rule-Based Peer-to-Peer Pricing in Residential PV-BES Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01680)
*Pablo Benalcazar,Maciej Kalka,Wilian Guamán,Jacek Kamiński*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了基于规则与基于强化学习的两种P2P电力交易定价机制。结果表明，在纯光伏配置下，基于规则的基准策略优于最优RL策略；而加入电池储能后，最优RL策略的社区节省从€734.23提升至€978.52。SDR形状定价优于乘数型参数化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究在住宅光伏社区P2P电力交易中，复杂的学习型定价机制是否真能超越简单直观的规则型定价机制，为社区能源交易定价策略的选择提供实证依据。

Method: 对比三类规则型基准（账单分摊、中间市场价、供需比定价）与基于Deep Q-Network的RL定价机制；RL策略在乘数型和可学习的SDR形状定价下评估，并设置固定参数的SDR变体作为非学习对照；评估指标涵盖社区节省及财务与运营指标。

Result: 纯光伏配置下规则型基准优于最优RL策略；加入储能后，最优RL策略的社区节省从€734.23增至€978.52；在两种配置下SDR形状定价均优于所考虑的乘数型参数化方式。

Conclusion: 规则型定价在与学习型定价直接比较的范围内仍具高度竞争力；储能显著改善学习型定价结果，但收益在家庭间分布不均。

Abstract: This paper compares rule-based and learning-based pricing mechanisms for peer-to-peer (P2P) electricity trading in residential photovoltaic communities. The rule-based benchmarks comprise bill-sharing as an ex post allocation mechanism, the mid-market rate, and supply-demand-ratio pricing. The reinforcement-learning (RL) formulation is implemented through a Deep Q-Network and evaluated under multiplier-based and learnable SDR-shaped pricing, with a fixed-parameter SDR variant as a non-learning control. Performance is assessed through community savings together with complementary financial and operational indicators. In the base PV-only configuration, the rule-based benchmarks outperform the best RL policy. With battery energy storage, evaluated for the RL policies only, community savings under the best RL policy increase from EUR 734.23 to EUR 978.52. Across the learning-based modes and in both configurations, SDR-shaped pricing outperforms the multiplier-based parameterization considered. The results indicate that rule-based pricing remains highly competitive wherever the two families are compared directly, and that storage substantially improves the learning-based outcomes under this accounting, while the distribution of benefits remains heterogeneous across households.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中位数均值方法在处理重尾和对抗性污染数据时虽已取得进展，但凸估计器在块污染模型下存在固有的鲁棒性局限，无法达到理论上最优的删块 oracle 常数。作者希望探索非凸方法突破这一凸类上的性能天花板。

Method: 作者首先推导了块污染模型下所有凸块 M-估计器的最坏情况鲁棒性常数下界；随后提出非凸的块-Lp（0&lt;p&lt;1）估计器族，推导了所有全局最小化器的有限样本确定性鲁棒性界，并分析了其目标函数的地形景观（所有局部极小值接近真值、无不良盆地），最后结合块级集中不等式推导亚高斯偏差界。

Result: 凸块 M-估计器的鲁棒性常数至少为 1/(1-2ε)，与经典中位数均值界匹配且无法被超越；块-Lp 估计器随 p 从 1 降至 0，其鲁棒性界连续逼近删块 oracle 常数，且在小 p 时全局最小化器与 oracle 一致；在仅需有限 2+δ 阶矩条件下获得亚高斯偏差界，并扩展到高维稳健均值估计与稀疏回归。

Conclusion: 该工作为鲁棒统计估计提供了理论支撑：凸方法在块污染模型下存在固有性能界限，非凸块-Lp 方法能够在保持良性景观（无坏局部极小）的前提下突破凸类的限制，逼近信息论意义上的最优鲁棒性常数，同时保留良好的统计效率。

Abstract: We revisit median-of-means estimation from a deterministic optimization viewpoint and develop a family of block-Lp estimators for robust learning with heavy-tailed and adversarially corrupted data. In a block contamination model with at least a fraction 1 minus epsilon of good blocks, we first show that every convex block M-estimator has worst-case robustness constant at least 1 divided by 1 minus 2 epsilon. This matches the classical median-of-means bound and proves that the trimmed-block oracle constant 1 divided by 1 minus epsilon cannot be attained within the convex class. We then introduce a nonconvex block-Lp family for p between 0 and 1 and derive finite-sample deterministic robustness bounds for all global minimizers. As p decreases from 1 toward 0, these bounds continuously approach the trimmed-block oracle constant. For sufficiently small p, the global minimizers coincide with those of the oracle under a mild separation condition. We also show that the block-Lp objectives have a benign landscape, with all local minima remaining close to the truth and no bad basins. Combining these results with block-level concentration yields sub-Gaussian deviation bounds under finite 2 plus delta moments and high-dimensional extensions to robust mean estimation and sparse regression.

Method: 提出端到端专业化流程：(1) 大规模题目筛选构建22,000道精选题目；(2) 合成推理轨迹生成；(3) 分别用SFT+RL训练Nemotron-3-Nano-CC (30B-A3B)和仅用SFT训练Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC (550B-A55B)；(4) 引入GenCorrect——一种基于反馈的测试时计算策略，迭代生成、评估和精化多样化解决方案。

Result: IOI 2025上：Nano-CC从130分经后训练提至291分，配合GenCorrect达到468分（超过金牌阈值438.3），Ultra-CC达到502分。基于此开发竞赛专用Ultra-CC系统，在IOI 2026真实竞赛条件（相同时间、互联网访问和提交限制）下前瞻性评估获得535.4/600分，超过金牌线361.12和人类最高分498.27。

Conclusion: 该工作首次证明AI系统能在IOI竞赛题目集上超过人类最高分参赛者，展示了结合大规模数据筛选、SFT、RL和GenCorrect反馈驱动测试时计算的端到端专业化流程在极限推理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Competitive programming has become a key test of large language model reasoning, with international competitions such as IOI and ICPC representing its most challenging settings. We present an end-to-end specialization pipeline combining large-scale problem curation, synthetic reasoning traces, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL). Using 22,000 curated problems, we train Nemotron-3-Nano-CC (30B-A3B) with SFT and RL and Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC (550B-A55B) with SFT alone. We further introduce GenCorrect, a feedback-driven test-time compute strategy that iteratively generates, evaluates, and refines diverse solutions. On IOI 2025, Nano-CC improves from 130 points to 291 after post-training and to 468 with GenCorrect, exceeding the gold threshold of 438.3 while Ultra-CC reaches 502. Guided by these results, we develop a competition-specific Ultra-CC system and evaluate it prospectively during IOI 2026. Under the same time, internet-access, and submission constraints as human contestants, it scores 535.4 out of 600, exceeding both the gold threshold of 361.12 and the top human score of 498.27. To our knowledge, this is the first AI system to outscore the highest-scoring human contestant on an IOI problem set.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向太赫兹（THz）无线数据中心的多层数字孪生（DT）框架，基于140/220/300 GHz三频段实测数据，构建了物理孪生、AI信道孪生、系统评估与操控四层架构，实现了高效的无线规划与实时优化。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI计算快速增长对数据中心互连提出高容量和灵活性需求，太赫兹通信因超宽带和高空间复用性成为未来无线数据中心的有力方案，但需要有效的规划和实时优化手段，数字孪生技术恰好满足这一需求。

Method: 提出测量驱动的多层DT框架：1）在140/220/300 GHz开展信道测量；2）联合优化几何、材料、天线和混合传播模型构建测量校准的物理孪生；3）开发视线（LoS）感知的隐式神经场构建AI信道孪生，实现接收功率和LoS概率的实时预测；4）基于重建的信道场进行覆盖和干扰的系统级评估。

Result: AI孪生相比现有神经场基线实现了更低的功率重建误差并保持实时推理能力；天花板安装的AP部署在10 dB SINR阈值下覆盖率超过90%。

Conclusion: 实验验证了所提出的DT框架在太赫兹无线数据中心规划与优化中的有效性，兼具高精度信道重建和实时推断能力。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI computing has driven increasing demands for flexible and high-capacity data-center interconnections. Owing to its ultra-wide bandwidth and high spatial reuse capability, terahertz (THz) communication has emerged as a promising solution for future wireless data centers, while digital twins (DTs) enable efficient wireless planning and real-time optimization. In this work, a measurement-driven multi-layer DT framework is proposed for THz wireless data centers, where the physical, channel, evaluation, and manipulation layers are progressively constructed from bottom to top. First, extensive channel measurements are conducted at 140, 220, and 300 GHz to characterize frequency-dependent propagation behaviors. Based on the tri-band measurements, a measurement-calibrated physical twin is established by jointly optimizing the geometry, material, antenna, and hybrid propagation models. On top of the physical twin, a line-of-sight (LoS)-aware implicit neural field is developed to construct an AI channel twin for efficient channel reconstruction. The proposed AI twin learns location-dependent channel statistics from the calibrated twin, enabling real-time prediction of received power and LoS probability. Building upon the reconstructed channel field, a system-level evaluation layer is derived to analyze coverage and interference for both AP-to-rack and rack-to-rack communications. Experimental results show that the proposed AI twin achieves lower power reconstruction error than existing neural-field baselines while maintaining real-time inference capability. Moreover, the ceiling-mounted AP deployment achieves over 90% coverage under a 10 dB signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) threshold, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed DT framework for THz wireless data-center planning and optimization.

</details>


### [20] [Generative Diffusion Surrogates with Analytical Variance Schedule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01705)
*Patrick Reichherzer,Gianluca Gregori,David N. Hosking,Subir Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将生成式扩散模型的加噪调度与物理传输系统的方差路径（均方位移）对齐的方法，使生成时间成为校准的传输时钟，从而无需中间时间物理数据即可模拟随机传输系统中的非高斯分布演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机传输系统的替代模型需要具有概率性、时间分辨且能表示非高斯分布结构。扩散模型具备这些性质，但其噪声调度通常是启发式选择的，缺乏物理时钟。而在传输问题中，方差（均方位移）往往可以从宏观理论或经验标度得知，即使完整分布未知。

Method: 将前向加噪速率设定为该方差的时间导数，从而将生成式时间转换为校准的传输时钟。方差路径通过构造得以保证，而学习的得分场则表示从入口数据继承的非高斯结构沿该路径如何被平滑化，不需要中间时间的物理传输数据。

Result: 在湍流等离子体中的弹道到扩散输运测试中，该替代模型能够匹配测试粒子分布，复现实验室测量的方差标度，并跟踪模拟的峰度演化，且无需调度调优，实现了校准的仿真和基于似然的推断。

Conclusion: 通过将物理系统的方差信息嵌入扩散模型的噪声调度，可以构建具有物理时钟校准能力的生成式传输替代模型，为随机传输系统的仿真与推断提供新的方法路径。

Abstract: Stochastic transport describes physical systems in which an initially structured distribution spreads under unresolved forcing, scattering, or heterogeneous media. Useful surrogates for such systems should be probabilistic, time-resolved, and able to represent non-Gaussian distributional structure. Generative diffusion models, which corrupt data with Gaussian noise and learn a reverse flow back to structured states, have these properties. Their noise schedules, however, are usually chosen heuristically: image and audio generation---the canonical use cases---provide no physical clock. In transport, by contrast, the variance, or mean-square displacement, is often known from macroscopic theory or empirical scaling even when the full distribution is not. Here we prescribe the forward noising rate as the time derivative of this variance, turning generative time into a calibrated transport clock. The variance path is enforced by construction, while the learned score field represents how non-Gaussian structure inherited from entrance data is smoothed along that path, requiring no intermediate-time physical transport data. For ballistic-to-diffusive transport in turbulent plasmas, the surrogate matches test-particle distributions, reproduces the laboratory-measured variance scale, and tracks the simulated kurtosis evolution without schedule tuning, enabling calibrated emulation and likelihood-based inference.

</details>


### [21] [RecKAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with a Learnable Recursive Polynomial Basis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01729)
*Amirhosein Azarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RecKAN，通过在KAN网络中引入二阶多项式递推来学习基函数本身（而非仅学习组合权重），该递推可恢复多种经典多项式族，在多个基准数据集上优于参数匹配的KAN基线模型，且学到的递推系数具有可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KAN变体虽然用可学习的单变量函数替换了固定标量权重，但其基函数仍是固定的（如B样条、切比雪夫多项式、小波、雅可比多项式），仅学习基上的组合权重。作者希望让基函数本身也成为可学习的一部分，从而突破固定经典多项式族的表达能力限制。

Method: 提出RecKAN，用二阶多项式递推R_{n+1}(x)=(ax^2+bx+c)R_n(x)+(dx+e)R_{n-1}(x)定义基函数，五个系数与网络联合学习。该递推能恢复切比雪夫（两种）、斐波那契、佩尔和雅各布斯塔尔多项式等经典族。作者证明在包含所有这些经典族的子族上，多项式度数随n线性增长，表明学习到的基可超越任何固定经典选择。

Result: 在涵盖图像、文本、生物医学时间序列分类和时间序列预测的多个基准数据集中，RecKAN在所有分类任务上优于三个参数匹配的KAN基线（切比雪夫、雅可比和样条基），在ETTh1预测基准上取得最低MSE。用作卷积骨干的分类头时，在Fashion MNIST、CIFAR-10和SVHN上比标准MLP头取得更高准确率。合成函数拟合基准上能追踪MLP欠拟合的高频振荡目标。学习到的递推系数可解释，在需要最多局部结构的任务上，训练使基偏离包含所有经典族的线性度数增长区域。

Conclusion: RecKAN通过学习基函数本身而非仅学习组合权重，在分类、预测和函数拟合等任务上全面优于参数匹配的KAN基线和MLP基线。递推系数的可解释性与理论分析一致，表明学习到的基能根据需要动态调整结构特性（如局部性），超越固定经典多项式族的表达能力。

Abstract: Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) replace the fixed scalar weights of a standard network with learnable univariate functions on each edge, but existing variants still fix the \emph{basis} that those functions are built from: B-splines, Chebyshev polynomials, wavelets, or Jacobi polynomials, and learn only the combination weights over it. We introduce RecKAN, which instead defines the basis itself by a second order polynomial recurrence, $R_{n+1}(x) = (ax^2+bx+c)R_n(x) + (dx+e)R_{n-1}(x)$, whose five coefficients are learned jointly with the network. We show this recurrence recovers several classical polynomial families including both kinds of Chebyshev polynomials, Fibonacci, Pell, and Jacobsthal polynomials as special cases, and prove that its degree grows linearly in $n$ exactly on the sub-family containing all of them, giving a concrete sense in which the learned basis can move beyond any fixed classical choice. Across multiple benchmark datasets spanning image, text, biomedical time series classification, and time series forecasting, RecKAN outperforms three parameter-matched KAN baselines (Chebyshev, Jacobi, and spline based) on all classification tasks and achieves the lowest MSE on the ETTh1 forecasting benchmark. Additionally, when used as a classifier head with a convolutional backbone, RecKAN achieves higher accuracy than standard MLP heads on Fashion MNIST, CIFAR-10, and SVHN. On a synthetic function fitting benchmark it tracks a sharply oscillatory target that a parameter comparable MLP under fits. We further show that the learned recurrence coefficients are interpretable: on the task requiring the most local structure, training moves the basis away from the linear degree growth regime that contains every classical family we identify, consistent with our theoretical analysis of what that structural shift enables.

</details>


### [22] [CAT-Flow: Curvature-Adaptive sTeps for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01746)
*Qinchan Li,Pedro Cisneros-Velarde,Keru Fu,Samuel Antunes Miranda,Sharan Vaswani,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种轻量级、无需训练的推理时自适应步长算法CAT-OV和CAT-OT，基于流匹配采样与梯度流之间的新颖联系，在不增加额外神经网络计算的前提下显著提升图像生成效率，可将达到同等质量所需的生成步数减少多达40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配框架虽然已成为生成建模的领先方案（如FLUX和Stable Diffusion 3.5），但其基于ODE的迭代采样过程存在根本性的效率瓶颈：生成质量对步长选择非常敏感，当前模型通常需要20至30步才能获得良好质量。

Method: 提出两种训练免费算法：CAT-OT通过对向量场的时间导数进行有限差分近似来估计曲率；CAT-OV通过对向量场的状态空间梯度来近似曲率。两种方法在推理时自适应调整步长，且不需要额外的神经网络函数评估，计算高效。在适当条件下，两种方法的截断误差界均为常数阶。

Result: 在四个文生图流匹配模型上，CAT-OV和CAT-OT在图像质量指标上优于现有的步长启发式方法，将达到可比质量所需的生成步数减少了多达40%。

Conclusion: 该工作通过建立流匹配采样与梯度流之间的理论联系，提出了无需训练的推理时自适应步长算法，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升了采样效率，为流匹配模型的加速采样提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Flow Matching has emerged as a leading framework for generative modeling, powering state-of-the-art systems such as FLUX and Stable Diffusion 3.5. However, the iterative nature of its ODE-based sampling process creates a fundamental efficiency bottleneck: the quality of generated samples is highly sensitive to the choice of step-sizes, and current models typically require 20 to 30 steps for good quality. In this work, we propose two lightweight, training-free algorithms, CAT-OV and CAT-OT that adapt step-sizes at inference time based on a novel connection between Flow Matching sampling and gradient flow. Our algorithms are computed efficiently by not requiring additional neural function evaluations. Specifically, CAT-OT estimates curvature over time via a finite-difference approximation of the time-derivative of the vector field, while CAT-OV approximates curvature over the state space via a gradient of the vector field. Under suitable conditions, both methods have truncation error bounds of constant order. Empirically, CAT-OV and CAT-OT outperform existing step-size heuristics in image quality metrics across four text- to-image Flow Matching models, reducing the number of generation steps required to reach comparable quality by up to 40%.

</details>


### [23] [A Study of Conditional Diffusion Models for Open-Loop Control under Dry Friction and Stiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01756)
*Eric Aislan Antonelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于动作序列的扩散模型（Action Diffusion）在具有干摩擦和静摩擦的点质量系统中的开环控制性能，并与随机采样和交叉熵方法（CEM）对比，结果表明条件扩散模型在低样本场景下能显著降低终端误差和卡顿步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型已成为规划和控制的生成式先验，但现有研究尚未充分探索其在具有干摩擦和静摩擦等非理想物理条件下的动作序列生成能力。此类系统中，有效控制信号仅占据动作序列空间中小而结构化的一小部分，传统采样方法效率低下。

Method: 采用紧凑的条件1D U-Net生成有界控制序列，以初始状态和目标状态为条件。与均匀随机采样、基于结构化数据集先验的随机采样以及交叉熵方法（CEM）进行对比实验。

Result: Action Diffusion在终端误差和卡顿步数两个指标上均优于对比方法，尤其在低样本（低预算）场景下优势更为显著。

Conclusion: 条件扩散模型能够通过条件约束和重组训练先验中的结构化控制原语，生成时间上连贯的控制序列，从而有效克服静摩擦，为状态到状态的开环控制提供了一种有效的生成机制。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently emerged as expressive generative priors for planning and control. This paper studies Action Diffusion, an action-sequence diffusion formulation used as an open-loop proposal distribution for a point-mass system with dry friction and stiction. In this benchmark, motion starts only when the applied input exceeds a static-friction threshold, so effective controls occupy a small and temporally structured subset of the action-sequence space. A compact conditional 1D U-Net generates bounded control sequences conditioned on initial and target states. We compare it with uniform random shooting, random shooting from the same structured dataset prior, and the Cross-Entropy Method (CEM). Results show that Action Diffusion reduces terminal error and stuck steps, especially in low-sample regimes. These results indicate that conditional diffusion provides an effective mechanism for generating temporally coherent control sequences that overcome stiction by conditioning and recombining structured control primitives from the training prior for state-to-state open-loop control.

</details>


### [24] [Toward Explainable and Policy-Aware AI for Carbon Credit Price Prediction: A Research Framework for Emerging Carbon Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01765)
*Summaiya Unnisa Begum,Mohammed Nadeem Ullah,Mohammed Abdul Ghani Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EPA-CarbonNet，一种融合碳市场价格序列与政策文本的六层深度网络架构，并在11年碳指数数据上测试，发现随机游走在五日均方根误差上优于模型，SHAP排序重采样一致性中等，政策注意力未匹配真实监管事件，仅方向准确率（58.6%）领先所有基线。研究以负面但可测量的结果，系统归纳了该领域的十个常见缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳市场为排放定价，但价格难以预测。现有研究集中于欧盟和中国碳排放权交易体系，将监管文本压缩为单一情绪分数，且报告精度时缺乏校准和解释稳定性。作者据此提炼出领域内反复出现的十个研究空白。

Method: 构建影响-可行性矩阵来系统化十个研究缺陷；设计EPA-CarbonNet六层架构，通过交叉注意力融合市场时间序列与政策文本，提供校准区间及基于政策的归因解释；在11年日度标普碳指数数据上开展实证测试。

Result: 模型五日均方根误差为0.0475，劣于随机游走的0.0365；SHAP的特征排序在不同重采样背景间的一致性系数仅为0.54；政策注意力与已记录的监管事件从未重合；但方向准确率达58.6%，优于全部基线。代码、数据文档与全部结果工件均已公开。

Conclusion: 该研究以“负面结果如实报告”的严谨姿态表明：即使采用精细设计的多层架构，碳价预测仍难以超越随机游走；政策文本注意力机制并未捕捉到真实监管信号，模型的可解释性与预测可靠性均有显著局限，提示该领域亟需更审慎的评估基准与解释验证方法。

Abstract: Carbon markets put a price on emissions, yet that price remains hard to forecast. Work in this area clusters on the EU and Chinese schemes, compresses regulatory text into a sentiment score, and reports accuracy without calibration or explanation stability. We distil ten recurring gaps into an impact-feasibility matrix and propose EPA-CarbonNet, a six-layer architecture that fuses market series with policy text by cross-attention and calibrated intervals alongside policy-attributed explanations. We then build and test it on eleven years of daily S and P carbon index data. The findings are largely negative, and reported as measured: a random walk beats the model on five-day RMSE (0.0365 against 0.0475), SHAP rankings agree at rho = 0.54 across resampled backgrounds, and policy attention never coincides with documented regulatory events. Directional accuracy, at 58.6 percent, leads every baseline. Code, data documentation and all result artifacts are available at https://github.com/Kimalice/Toward-Explainable-and-Policy-Aware-AI-for-Carbon-Credit-Price-Prediction

</details>


### [25] [Emergence of Fibrations, Compression, and Symmetry Breaking in Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01768)
*Osvaldo M Velarde,Lucas C Parra,Alireza Hashemi,Hernan A Makse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明深度神经网络学习过程中会产生图论中称为纤维化和覆盖的局部对称性，这些覆盖对称性是随机梯度下降的稳定吸引子。利用这些对称性可将模型压缩至原始大小的17%而不损失性能，且通过受控打破覆盖对称性可克服学习可塑性丧失问题，在持续学习任务中达到最先进水平，为可解释AI和高效推理提供了新基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工神经网络常被视为强大但不透明的黑箱，缺乏可解释性和理论指导。作者希望从图论对称性角度揭示深度学习的内在结构，将黑箱转化为可解释的着色图，从而提升模型压缩效率和实现终身学习。

Method: 作者从理论上证明了覆盖对称性是随机梯度下降的稳定吸引子，并通过实验验证了该理论在多层感知机、卷积网络、循环网络和Transformer等多种主流架构中的普适性。基于发现的对称性提出模型压缩方法，并通过受控打破对称性来恢复学习可塑性。

Result: 覆盖对称性在多种主流网络架构中普遍涌现。利用这些对称性可将模型压缩至原来大小的17%而不损失性能。受控打破对称性克服了可塑性丧失问题，在持续学习基准上达到最先进的性能水平。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于对称性的AI系统提供了新的理论基础，能够将黑箱网络转化为可解释的着色图结构，实现更高效的推理和终身学习能力，标志着连接深度学习与图论对称性理论的重要进展。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks are often regarded as powerful yet opaque black boxes. Here, we demonstrate that learning in deep neural networks generates local symmetries known in graph theory as fibrations and coverings. We prove that covering symmetries are stable attractors of stochastic gradient descent. Consistent with this theory, we report the emergence of covering symmetries across major network architectures, including multilayer, convolutional, recurrent, and transformer networks. Exploiting these symmetries enables drastic model compression - reducing networks to 17% of their original size without sacrificing performance. Furthermore, controlled breaking of covering symmetry overcomes the loss of plasticity, achieving state-of-the-art performance in continual learning. The theoretical results provide a new foundation for AI systems based on symmetries that convert black boxes into interpretable colored graphs and enable more efficient inference and lifelong learning.

</details>


### [26] [D-FROST: Decentralized Federated pRompt-tuning via Optimal tranSporT for Non-IID and Imbalanced Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01802)
*Quan Minh Nguyen,Hoang M. Ngo,Trong Nghia Hoang,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了去中心化联邦学习（DFL）中的提示微调问题，提出基于最优传输的D-FROST算法，通过Wasserstein优化框架解决了异构客户端提示集合不对齐的问题，并提供了理论与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示微调在去中心化联邦学习中具有参数高效的天然优势，但异构本地数据学到的提示集合缺乏索引级对齐，使得标准的去中心化平均方法不再适用。目前缺乏针对DFL中提示微调的理论保证与实用算法。

Method: 将去中心化提示微调形式化为提示测度上的Wasserstein优化问题，捕捉提示的集合值结构；提出D-FROST算法，通过基于最优传输的匹配将邻域提示合并为紧凑的代表性提示集。

Result: 理论上界定了客户端间的Wasserstein共识误差，并建立了网络级提示重心向驻点邻域收敛的保证；在异构客户端数据上的实验证明了D-FROST在去中心化提示微调中的有效性。

Conclusion: D-FROST为去中心化联邦学习中的提示微调提供了首个理论上有保证的算法框架，有效解决了异构数据下提示不对齐的挑战，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Prompt tuning provides a parameter-efficient way to adapt foundation models (FMs) by freezing the pretrained backbone and updating only a small set of learnable prompts. This property makes prompt tuning especially suitable for decentralized federated learning (DFL), where exchanging full-model updates can be prohibitively expensive. However, prompt tuning in DFL introduces new challenges. Prompt sets learned from heterogeneous local data may not be index-wise aligned, making standard decentralized averaging unsuitable. In addition, the algorithm should be theoretically guaranteed to achieve consensus and make progress toward the shared objective. In this work, we provide the first study of prompt tuning in DFL. We formulate decentralized prompt tuning as a Wasserstein-based optimization problem over prompt measures, which captures the set-valued structure of prompts. We then propose D-FROST, an optimal-transport-based (OT-based) decentralized prompt-tuning algorithm that merges neighborhood prompts into compact representative prompt sets through transportation-based matching. We further analyze D-FROST by bounding the Wasserstein consensus error across clients, and establishing convergence of the network-level prompt barycenter to a neighborhood of stationarity. Experiments under heterogeneous client data demonstrate the effectiveness of D-FROST for decentralized prompt tuning.

</details>


### [27] [hLLM: Single Pass Decoding for Generative Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01807)
*Emil Laftchiev,Prachi Agrawal,Moe Kayali,Bixing Yan,Qi Xu,Zijie Lei,Chen Qiu,Zhi Hua,Ke Li,Luke Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出hLLM（匈牙利LLM），一种专门针对排序任务的高效解码策略。通过从LLM的预填充隐藏状态中读取N×K的项目-位置分数矩阵，并用匈牙利算法求解最优二分匹配，所有N个序数可在O(1)次前向传播内完成解码，端到端推理速度达到28毫秒，相比教师模型加速64倍，同时保持相当的排序质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在生成式排序任务中表现出色，但自回归解码每次仅生成一个token，需要N次顺序前向传播，推理效率极低。而排序任务实际只需输出N个序数来描述排列顺序，这种受限的排列结构输出格式应该可以用比从左到右生成更高效的解码策略来处理。

Method: hLLM的核心方法包括：1) 利用LLM的预填充隐藏状态，通过轻量级自注意力头读取N×K的项目-位置分数矩阵；2) 将该矩阵通过匈牙利算法求解最优二分分配，直接得到有效的排列（由构造保证合法性，无需修复步骤）；3) 结合LoRA微调与教师排序蒸馏作为训练信号；4) 对架构、训练信号和骨干网络适配进行系统性消融研究。

Result: LoRA微调结合教师排序蒸馏的方法实现了28毫秒的端到端推理，相比教师模型加速64倍，同时保持了与教师相当的排序质量。文章还提供了完整的消融实验，分解了架构、训练信号和骨干适配各自的贡献。

Conclusion: hLLM将生成式排序与组合优化联系起来，证明排序任务的排列结构化输出格式可以通过O(1)次前向传播完成解码，为实时排序及其他O(1)解码机制开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve state-of-the-art generative ranking quality, but the ranking they produce must be decoded, and autoregressive decoding spends one sequential forward pass per emitted token. We observe that the only tokens a ranker must emit are the $N$ ordinal values naming the items in ranked order, and that this narrow, permutation-structured output format admits decoding strategies which are much more efficient than left-to-right generation. We introduce hLLM (Hungarian LLM), a format-specialized decoding strategy that decodes all $N$ ordinals in $O(1)$ forward passes. hLLM reads an $N \times K$ item-position score matrix off the LLM's prefill hidden states with a lightweight self-attention head, then decodes the ordinals as the optimal bipartite assignment of that matrix via the Hungarian algorithm, yielding a valid permutation by construction rather than by repair. Through a systematic study of training signals and backbone adaptation, we show that LoRA-based fine-tuning combined with teacher ranking distillation reaches 28 ms end-to-end inference, a speed-up of $64\times$ while maintaining ranking quality on par with the teacher. We provide a complete ablation decomposing the contributions of architecture, training signal, and backbone adaptation. Our framework connects generative ranking to combinatorial optimization, opening a path toward other $O(1)$-decode mechanisms for real-time ranking.

</details>


### [28] [Import What You Need: Learning When and How to Augment EHR Graphs with External Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01839)
*Chen Chen,Mohsen Nayebi Kerdabadi,Dongjie Wang,Mei Liu,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReTA框架，利用强化学习实现每次就诊的自适应知识图谱拓扑增强，从Soft Import、Hard Import和Skip三种动作中按预算选择最优增强策略，在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上超越多个强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子病历预测受限于患者轨迹的稀疏性和不规则性，现有知识增强方法采用固定的、与上下文无关的拓扑增强方式，无法根据患者状态动态调整知识导入策略。

Method: ReTA首先构建离线精炼的知识图谱模板池，然后通过强化学习学习一个策略，每次就诊从Soft Import（增强节点特征）、Hard Import（嫁接紧凑知识子图形成消息传递捷径）和Skip（跳过增强）三种动作中选择；采用解耦编码器分别处理语义和结构信号，并通过自适应门控融合来稳定学习。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上针对诊断预测、死亡率和再入院任务，ReTA一致优于强基线，且保持高效性，可跨数据集和知识图谱迁移，产生可解释的增强模式，在稀疏监督下增益显著。

Conclusion: 动态决策知识导入的优势在于在提升精度的同时控制成本，验证了预算意识的自适应拓扑增强在电子病历预测中的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Longitudinal prediction from electronic health records (EHRs) is limited by the sparsity and irregularity in patient trajectories, and knowledge augmentation with external knowledge graphs (KGs) offers a promising way to alleviate these issues. However, most existing methods perform fixed, context-agnostic topology augmentation by adding the same KG nodes and edges regardless of a patient's evolving state. We propose ReTA, a Reinforcement learning-based dynamic Topology Augmentation framework that casts KG import as a per-visit, budget-aware policy. ReTA first constructs an offline refined pool of KG-grounded templates, then learns a policy to select one augment action per visit from three options: Soft Import, which enriches node features without modifying graph topology, Hard Import, which grafts a compact KG subgraph onto the visit graph to create message-passing shortcuts, and Skip, which leaves the visit unaugmented when the base encoder is already confident. To stabilize learning, ReTA employs a decoupled encoder that processes semantic and structural signals in separate channels and fuses them via adaptive gating. Experiments on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV across diagnosis prediction, mortality, and readmission show that ReTA consistently outperforms strong baselines while remaining efficient, transfers across datasets and knowledge graphs, and yields interpretable augmentation patterns. The robust gains under sparse supervision highlight the advantage of ReTA's dynamic decision to import knowledge, boosting accuracy while curbing costs.

</details>


### [29] [OutageDiT: A Generative Foundation Model for Power Outage Forecasting and Scenario Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01896)
*Yunqin Zhu,Feng Qiu,Yao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OutageDiT，一个在美国全国停电和天气数据上训练的基础模型，用于生成15分钟分辨率、七天时长的停电轨迹，并结合点预测、不确定性量化和条件事件模拟于一体，显著提升了预测精度和场景质量，并支持对未见过区域的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 停电规划需要在事件发生前生成场景，但严重事件是稀有的，任何单一区域的数据都缺乏足够的极端停电和恢复模式样本，传统方法难以在有限数据下同时捕捉幅值、时间和持续时长上的不确定性并保持时间依赖性。

Method: 条件编码器在每次预测时仅处理一次历史上下文和已知的未来协变量，随后浅层流解码器复用由此产生的时间对齐状态来生成完整轨迹；模型基于美国范围内的停电和天气记录进行训练，实现多任务统一的深度生成框架。

Result: 在停电预测基准上，OutageDiT相比强基线显著提升了预测准确性和场景质量，并展现出对未覆盖区域的零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: 条件性停电模拟可以弥合停电预测与不确定性条件下运营规划之间的鸿沟，将停电模拟定位为从预测走向实际规划操作的有效桥梁。

Abstract: Power-outage planning requires scenarios before an event occurs. These scenarios must represent uncertainty in magnitude, timing, and duration while preserving temporal dependence. However, severe events are rare, and data from any single region contain few examples of extreme outage and restoration patterns. To address this challenge, we introduce OutageDiT, a foundation model for generating seven-day outage trajectories at quarter-hour resolution, trained on outage and weather records across the United States. Specifically, a condition encoder processes the historical context and known future covariates once per forecast, and a shallow flow decoder reuses the resulting horizon-aligned states to generate complete trajectories. The resulting samples support point forecasting, uncertainty quantification, and conditional event simulation within one deep generative model. Across outage forecasting benchmarks, OutageDiT improves forecast accuracy and scenario quality over strong baselines and supports zero-shot transfer to held-out regions. Together, these results position conditional outage simulation as a bridge from outage forecasting to operational planning under uncertainty.

</details>


### [30] [CRISP: Cliff-awaRe Input-adaptive Sparse Prefilling with Structural-Mass-Motivated Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01925)
*Huu Huy Nguyen,Chien Van Nguyen,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Linh Ngo Van,Jieyang Chen,Thien Huu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRISP（Cliff-awaRe Input-adaptive Sparse Prefilling），一种输入自适应的稀疏注意力预填充方法，通过结构代理C_struct替代JSD路由以减少开销，并提出基于噪声底限的sink-aware阈值来消除长上下文中的O(n)背景噪声累积。在InfiniteBench、RULER和LongBench基准上，CRISP在检索任务上超越稠密注意力，获得最高+28.0个百分点的提升，并在512k token下实现最高5.30倍的注意力加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理的注意力预填充阶段计算量随序列长度二次增长，成为严重瓶颈。现有稀疏注意力方法或依赖固定模式缺乏输入适应性，或依赖间接路由代理且预算分配机制忽视了softmax后的质量分布层次结构，需要一种既能动态适应输入又能高效消除噪声开销的稀疏注意力方法。

Method: CRISP识别并解决动态路由范式中的两个结构挑战：(1) 提出C_struct结构代理，直接从代理注意力图的结构中读取路由决策，替代JSD路由，消除了池化矩阵乘法和KL散度开销；(2) 形式化post-softmax质量悬崖问题，证明严格的累积覆盖率阈值在长上下文中会累积O(n)背景噪声，据此提出基于噪声底限的sink-aware阈值机制。

Result: 在InfiniteBench、RULER和LongBench上对两个模型家族进行验证，CRISP是整体最强的稀疏方法，在检索密集型基准上达到或超过精确稠密注意力，相对基线最多恢复+28.0个百分点的检索任务性能，在512k token长度下实现最高5.30倍的注意力加速。这些提升主要源于选择过程中的O(n)噪声消除同时保持结构完整性。

Conclusion: CRISP通过结构代理路由和噪声底限感知的阈值机制，成功解决了动态稀疏注意力中路由开销和噪声累积两大结构性挑战，证明了在保持检索性能的同时实现长上下文注意力加速的可行性。

Abstract: The attention prefilling phase of long-context LLM inference scales quadratically, making self-attention a severe computational bottleneck. Traditional sparse attention methods mitigate this through fixed patterns or offline profiling, but lack the flexibility to adapt to input-dependent attention structure. Recent dynamic methods address this by routing heads to sparse patterns in real-time, but rely on indirect routing proxies with overhead and budget allocation mechanisms that overlook the post-softmax mass hierarchy. We present CRISP (Cliff-awaRe Input-adaptive Sparse Prefilling), which identifies and addresses two structural challenges in this dynamic routing paradigm. First, we show that the routing decision can be read directly off the structure of the proxy attention map. We replace the Jensen-Shannon Divergence (JSD) routing with C_struct, a structural proxy that measures mass at Vertical-Slash compatible positions and reproduces JSD's routing decisions while eliminating both the pooled matmul and subsequent KL divergence overhead. Second, we formalize the post-softmax mass cliff and demonstrate theoretically that strictly cumulative coverage thresholds accumulate O(n) background noise at long contexts. CRISP navigates this via a sink-aware threshold grounded in the noise floor. Empirically, across InfiniteBench, RULER and LongBench on two model families, CRISP is the strongest sparse method overall and matches or exceeds exact dense attention on retrieval-heavy benchmarks, recovering up to +28.0 pp on retrieval tasks over baselines and achieving up to a 5.30x attention speedup at 512k tokens, driven primarily by our O(n) noise elimination during selection while preserving structural integrity.

</details>


### [31] [OR-Transformer: Scaling Real-Time Decision-Making to 1,000 Items](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01933)
*Shuze Daniel Liu,David Simchi-Levi,Claire Chen,Chutong Gao,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OR-Transformer，一种用于大规模随机需求下联合补货问题的深度强化学习框架。其采用物品置换等变的Transformer架构和路径梯度训练，在高达1024个库存物品的规模下，性能随规模增长持续超越基于学习和滚动时域MILP的基线方法，且在线决策时间相比MILP求解器降低超过400万倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代供应链运营需要在数千个异构物品间协调补货，面临相关随机需求、异构前置时间和共享固定订购成本，导致观测空间超过10^4维。在此规模下，滚动时域随机混合整数线性规划（MILP）求解速度过慢，而标准强化学习方法在高维动作空间中的信用分配面临严峻挑战。

Method: 提出OR-Transformer：一个基于深度强化学习的联合补货框架，核心包括（1）物品置换等变的Transformer网络架构，以应对高维异构物品的协调问题；（2）通过库存动力学的路径梯度训练方法，实现高效稳定的策略优化。

Result: 在最高1024个库存物品的各类问题规模下，OR-Transformer随规模增大大幅超越基于学习的基线和滚动时域MILP基线；在线决策时间相比MILP求解器减少了超过400万倍，支撑了实时大规模深度强化学习在供应链运营中的部署。

Conclusion: OR-Transformer成功将深度强化学习扩展到大规模供应链补货场景，在决策质量和实时性两方面均显著优于传统MILP方法，为实际供应链运营中的实时大规模决策提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Modern supply chain operations can require coordinating replenishment across thousands of heterogeneous items under correlated stochastic demand, heterogeneous lead times, and shared fixed ordering costs, yielding observation spaces exceeding $10^4$ dimensions. At this scale, rolling-horizon stochastic mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs) become prohibitively slow, while standard reinforcement learning (RL) methods face increasingly challenging credit assignment in high-dimensional action spaces. We introduce OR-Transformer, a deep reinforcement learning framework for joint replenishment under stochastic demand, with an item-permutation-equivariant Transformer architecture and pathwise-gradient training through the inventory dynamics. Across problem sizes up to 1,024 inventory items, OR-Transformer increasingly outperforms learning-based and rolling-horizon MILP baselines as scale grows. It also reduces online decision-making time by over 4 million times relative to MILP solvers, enabling real-time, large-scale deep RL in supply chain operations.

</details>

Abstract: Bitcoin's pseudonymous nature makes it challenging to analyze user-level activity, since a single user may control multiple identifiers (addresses). Existing heuristic-based methods attempt to identify addresses belonging to the same user, but they often produce flat cluster assignments with limited modularity and are prone to errors such as merging different users together. In this work, we propose a method for refining heuristic-obtained clusters by grounding our clustering on contrastive embeddings yielded by graph neural networks. Our contributions are threefold: (i) we release a publicly available dataset of Bitcoin transaction graphs containing a substantial number of clusters; (ii) we propose a methodology for learning address embeddings consistent with heuristics, and back it up with theoretical guiding intuitions; (iii) through hierarchical clustering, we enable a finer analysis of heuristic clusters and provide a quantitative criterion for flagging suspicious merges.

</details>

Abstract: Compact instruction-following rerankers are attractive for deployment, but conventional distillation pipelines typically train students by offline imitation of teacher outputs on a fixed set of examples, constraining supervision to the teacher's observed ranking space. In Stage 2, a compact 1B student samples rankings from its own policy and receives soft teacher-derived rewards on those rankings, coupling student exploration with knowledge transfer.

Controlled comparisons against offline pairwise RankNet KD and on-policy GKD show that neither changing the offline distillation objective nor moving teacher-distribution matching on-policy reproduces the performance of reward-based on-policy distillation over student-sampled rankings. The advantage persists on MAIR-Full: across all 126 tasks and 9,356 queries, the proposed method obtains the highest task-macro point estimates among the evaluated distillation variants, reaching 0.6808 nDCG@6 and 0.7865 MRR@6. It also exceeds two released 7B RL-trained rerankers on the comparable MAIR-11 evaluation, while the same Stage 2 training procedure consistently improves three architecturally distinct alternative student backbones.
